        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Add a Project + ProjectTimeline abstraction to ArtAgents so that orchestrator/executor runs are organized under named, persistent projects with a canonical project-level timeline that composes clips from many sources and runs.

ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS (settled — do not relitigate):

1. Projects live at a fixed root: ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/ (overridable via ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT env var). Each project is a directory keyed by slug.

2. Project layout on disk:
   <projects_root>/
     <slug>/
       project.json
       timeline.json
       sources/<source_id>/
         source.json
         analysis/
       runs/<run_id>/
         run.json
         timeline.json
         assets.json
         metadata.json

3. The project timeline is a NEW file format (placements that reference sources OR run-clips), distinct from the existing flat hype.timeline.json. ArtAgents-only — the shared @banodoco/timeline-schema is NOT modified. A materialize step composes the project timeline + referenced run/source data into a standard TimelineConfig + AssetRegistry.

4. Placement schema with id, track, at, from, to, source ref (kind:source|run), entrance, exit, transition, effects, params.

5. Orchestrator/executor bodies are NOT modified. The orchestrator runner gains an optional --project arg that mints run_id, sets out path, runs unchanged, then writes run.json on completion. --out continues to work for ad-hoc runs.

6. Agent UX: --project required for state-changing commands; every command echoes a project header; show prints a tree; errors name the next command.

WORK BREAKDOWN — 4 chunks:
CHUNK 1: Project skeleton + CLI (artagents/core/project/ module with paths/schema/project/source/run/timeline/cli, register in setup_cli.py).
CHUNK 2: ProjectTimeline + materialize source-only + placement editing CLI.
CHUNK 3: Run integration (orchestrator runner gains project flag, hype run.py adds --project, materialize learns kind:run placements, place-run CLI).
CHUNK 4: Tests, polish, doctor command.

OUT OF SCOPE: changes to @banodoco/timeline-schema; modifications to executor bodies; reigh-app integration; multiple named timelines per project UX; project templates/cloning; --copy mode; rich placement primitives (split/ripple/snap); current-project global state.

INVARIANTS: existing executor outputs byte-for-byte unchanged when invoked via --out; materialized output validates against existing validate_timeline(); existing render executor consumes materialized timeline unchanged.

KEY TOUCHPOINTS: setup_cli.py (subparser); core/orchestrator/runner.py (project flag, run_id, run.json writer); orchestrators/hype/run.py (argparse --project alt to --out); timeline.py (materialize_project_timeline); new artagents/core/project/; new tests/test_project_*.py.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Add a Project + ProjectTimeline abstraction to ArtAgents so that orchestrator/executor runs are organized under named, persistent projects with a canonical project-level timeline that composes clips from many sources and runs.

ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS (settled — do not relitigate):

1. Projects live at a fixed root: ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/ (overridable via ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT env var). Each project is a directory keyed by slug.

2. Project layout on disk:
   <projects_root>/
     <slug>/
       project.json
       timeline.json
       sources/<source_id>/
         source.json
         analysis/
       runs/<run_id>/
         run.json
         timeline.json
         assets.json
         metadata.json

3. The project timeline is a NEW file format (placements that reference sources OR run-clips), distinct from the existing flat hype.timeline.json. ArtAgents-only — the shared @banodoco/timeline-schema is NOT modified. A materialize step composes the project timeline + referenced run/source data into a standard TimelineConfig + AssetRegistry.

4. Placement schema with id, track, at, from, to, source ref (kind:source|run), entrance, exit, transition, effects, params.

5. Orchestrator/executor bodies are NOT modified. The orchestrator runner gains an optional --project arg that mints run_id, sets out path, runs unchanged, then writes run.json on completion. --out continues to work for ad-hoc runs.

6. Agent UX: --project required for state-changing commands; every command echoes a project header; show prints a tree; errors name the next command.

WORK BREAKDOWN — 4 chunks:
CHUNK 1: Project skeleton + CLI (artagents/core/project/ module with paths/schema/project/source/run/timeline/cli, register in setup_cli.py).
CHUNK 2: ProjectTimeline + materialize source-only + placement editing CLI.
CHUNK 3: Run integration (orchestrator runner gains project flag, hype run.py adds --project, materialize learns kind:run placements, place-run CLI).
CHUNK 4: Tests, polish, doctor command.

OUT OF SCOPE: changes to @banodoco/timeline-schema; modifications to executor bodies; reigh-app integration; multiple named timelines per project UX; project templates/cloning; --copy mode; rich placement primitives (split/ripple/snap); current-project global state.

INVARIANTS: existing executor outputs byte-for-byte unchanged when invoked via --out; materialized output validates against existing validate_timeline(); existing render executor consumes materialized timeline unchanged.

KEY TOUCHPOINTS: setup_cli.py (subparser); core/orchestrator/runner.py (project flag, run_id, run.json writer); orchestrators/hype/run.py (argparse --project alt to --out); timeline.py (materialize_project_timeline); new artagents/core/project/; new tests/test_project_*.py.

    "Hype final artifacts may live under `<run_root>/briefs/<brief_slug>/`; artifact mirroring must search explicit roots first, then run root, then known brief slug, then exactly one matching `briefs/*` set.",

    "Placement `from` and `to` directly override copied/materialized clip timing fields; richer split/ripple/snap semantics are out of scope.",

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Add a Project + ProjectTimeline abstraction to ArtAgents so that orchestrator/executor runs are organized under named, persistent projects with a canonical project-level timeline that composes clips from many sources and runs.

ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS (settled — do not relitigate):

1. Projects live at a fixed root: ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/ (overridable via ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT env var). Each project is a directory keyed by slug.

2. Project layout on disk:
   <projects_root>/
     <slug>/
       project.json
       timeline.json
       sources/<source_id>/
         source.json
         analysis/
       runs/<run_id>/
         run.json
         timeline.json
         assets.json
         metadata.json

3. The project timeline is a NEW file format (placements that reference sources OR run-clips), distinct from the existing flat hype.timeline.json. ArtAgents-only — the shared @banodoco/timeline-schema is NOT modified. A materialize step composes the project timeline + referenced run/source data into a standard TimelineConfig + AssetRegistry.

4. Placement schema with id, track, at, from, to, source ref (kind:source|run), entrance, exit, transition, effects, params.

5. Orchestrator/executor bodies are NOT modified. The orchestrator runner gains an optional --project arg that mints run_id, sets out path, runs unchanged, then writes run.json on completion. --out continues to work for ad-hoc runs.

6. Agent UX: --project required for state-changing commands; every command echoes a project header; show prints a tree; errors name the next command.

WORK BREAKDOWN — 4 chunks:
CHUNK 1: Project skeleton + CLI (artagents/core/project/ module with paths/schema/project/source/run/timeline/cli, register in setup_cli.py).
CHUNK 2: ProjectTimeline + materialize source-only + placement editing CLI.
CHUNK 3: Run integration (orchestrator runner gains project flag, hype run.py adds --project, materialize learns kind:run placements, place-run CLI).
CHUNK 4: Tests, polish, doctor command.

OUT OF SCOPE: changes to @banodoco/timeline-schema; modifications to executor bodies; reigh-app integration; multiple named timelines per project UX; project templates/cloning; --copy mode; rich placement primitives (split/ripple/snap); current-project global state.

INVARIANTS: existing executor outputs byte-for-byte unchanged when invoked via --out; materialized output validates against existing validate_timeline(); existing render executor consumes materialized timeline unchanged.

KEY TOUCHPOINTS: setup_cli.py (subparser); core/orchestrator/runner.py (project flag, run_id, run.json writer); orchestrators/hype/run.py (argparse --project alt to --out); timeline.py (materialize_project_timeline); new artagents/core/project/; new tests/test_project_*.py.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Add a Project + ProjectTimeline abstraction to ArtAgents so that orchestrator/executor runs are organized under named, persistent projects with a canonical project-level timeline that composes clips from many sources and runs.

ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS (settled — do not relitigate):

1. Projects live at a fixed root: ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/ (overridable via ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT env var). Each project is a directory keyed by slug.

2. Project layout on disk:
   <projects_root>/
     <slug>/
       project.json
       timeline.json
       sources/<source_id>/
         source.json
         analysis/
       runs/<run_id>/
         run.json
         timeline.json
         assets.json
         metadata.json

3. The project timeline is a NEW file format (placements that reference sources OR run-clips), distinct from the existing flat hype.timeline.json. ArtAgents-only — the shared @banodoco/timeline-schema is NOT modified. A materialize step composes the project timeline + referenced run/source data into a standard TimelineConfig + AssetRegistry.

4. Placement schema with id, track, at, from, to, source ref (kind:source|run), entrance, exit, transition, effects, params.

5. Orchestrator/executor bodies are NOT modified. The orchestrator runner gains an optional --project arg that mints run_id, sets out path, runs unchanged, then writes run.json on completion. --out continues to work for ad-hoc runs.

6. Agent UX: --project required for state-changing commands; every command echoes a project header; show prints a tree; errors name the next command.

WORK BREAKDOWN — 4 chunks:
CHUNK 1: Project skeleton + CLI (artagents/core/project/ module with paths/schema/project/source/run/timeline/cli, register in setup_cli.py).
CHUNK 2: ProjectTimeline + materialize source-only + placement editing CLI.
CHUNK 3: Run integration (orchestrator runner gains project flag, hype run.py adds --project, materialize learns kind:run placements, place-run CLI).
CHUNK 4: Tests, polish, doctor command.

OUT OF SCOPE: changes to @banodoco/timeline-schema; modifications to executor bodies; reigh-app integration; multiple named timelines per project UX; project templates/cloning; --copy mode; rich placement primitives (split/ripple/snap); current-project global state.

INVARIANTS: existing executor outputs byte-for-byte unchanged when invoked via --out; materialized output validates against existing validate_timeline(); existing render executor consumes materialized timeline unchanged.

KEY TOUCHPOINTS: setup_cli.py (subparser); core/orchestrator/runner.py (project flag, run_id, run.json writer); orchestrators/hype/run.py (argparse --project alt to --out); timeline.py (materialize_project_timeline); new artagents/core/project/; new tests/test_project_*.py.

  {

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Add a Project + ProjectTimeline abstraction to ArtAgents so that orchestrator/executor runs are organized under named, persistent projects with a canonical project-level timeline that composes clips from many sources and runs.

ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS (settled — do not relitigate):

1. Projects live at a fixed root: ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/ (overridable via ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT env var). Each project is a directory keyed by slug.

2. Project layout on disk:
   <projects_root>/
     <slug>/
       project.json
       timeline.json
       sources/<source_id>/
         source.json
         analysis/
       runs/<run_id>/
         run.json
         timeline.json
         assets.json
         metadata.json

3. The project timeline is a NEW file format (placements that reference sources OR run-clips), distinct from the existing flat hype.timeline.json. ArtAgents-only — the shared @banodoco/timeline-schema is NOT modified. A materialize step composes the project timeline + referenced run/source data into a standard TimelineConfig + AssetRegistry.

4. Placement schema with id, track, at, from, to, source ref (kind:source|run), entrance, exit, transition, effects, params.

5. Orchestrator/executor bodies are NOT modified. The orchestrator runner gains an optional --project arg that mints run_id, sets out path, runs unchanged, then writes run.json on completion. --out continues to work for ad-hoc runs.

6. Agent UX: --project required for state-changing commands; every command echoes a project header; show prints a tree; errors name the next command.

WORK BREAKDOWN — 4 chunks:
CHUNK 1: Project skeleton + CLI (artagents/core/project/ module with paths/schema/project/source/run/timeline/cli, register in setup_cli.py).
CHUNK 2: ProjectTimeline + materialize source-only + placement editing CLI.
CHUNK 3: Run integration (orchestrator runner gains project flag, hype run.py adds --project, materialize learns kind:run placements, place-run CLI).
CHUNK 4: Tests, polish, doctor command.

OUT OF SCOPE: changes to @banodoco/timeline-schema; modifications to executor bodies; reigh-app integration; multiple named timelines per project UX; project templates/cloning; --copy mode; rich placement primitives (split/ripple/snap); current-project global state.

INVARIANTS: existing executor outputs byte-for-byte unchanged when invoked via --out; materialized output validates against existing validate_timeline(); existing render executor consumes materialized timeline unchanged.

KEY TOUCHPOINTS: setup_cli.py (subparser); core/orchestrator/runner.py (project flag, run_id, run.json writer); orchestrators/hype/run.py (argparse --project alt to --out); timeline.py (materialize_project_timeline); new artagents/core/project/; new tests/test_project_*.py.
